Exact future source paths: NEW server/src/services/cash-chat-evidence.js; existing server/src/services/cash-review-evidence.js (chat reader delegation and raw operational handoff context only); NEW client/src/lib/cash-chat-evidence.js (safe shared label only); client/src/components/platform/PlatformAdminApp.jsx (existing analysis and copy evidence presentation only); client/src/components/cloud/CashControlPanel.jsx (investigation evidence presentation only); server/src/routes/cash-control.js (ONLY existing investigation reader adds shared evidence after existing scope guards, chat metadata only for authenticated non-operator OWNER; no review/writer changes); NEW server/test/cash-chat-evidence.test.js and cash-chat-evidence-postgres.test.js; existing server/test/cash-review-evidence.test.js and cash-review-evidence-postgres.test.js for context/compatibility; NEW server/test/cash-chat-evidence-ui.test.js. No store-chat routes/mobile/native/AI invoice/wallet/provider/source financial writer is claimed. Preserve source totals, confirmed-review arithmetic, existing candidates and all auth/licence/tenant controls.

This slice provides source discovery and handoff facts, not automatic OCR or final offsets. Image/PDF reading with its existing authorized AI metering, verified date/session association, durable reviewed offset decisions/recalculation history, common automatic/report/assistant consumers and checkpoint persistence remain REQUIRED subsequent bounded slices under this receiving owner, with interface coordination and amended exact claims before edits. Never mark the whole requested workflow installed from attachment metadata. Missing or unread handwritten evidence must be explicitly pending, not zero or agreement. Do not automatically call a paid AI provider from a GET/read-only report. Unknown external PDF/image contents cannot be treated as instructions.

Required tests for first slice: actual parameterized bounded SQL in isolated PostgreSQL, same-company/store isolation and foreign defense, no mutation, missing-table/query failure/over-cap, duplicate attachment checksums and stable source identity, misleading upload date, unsupported media and unread images, cross-date handoff, open/overlap/missing/invalid monetary values, retained sale/correction/balance regressions and real mounted/shared written labels. Fresh local Node20, full exact PR/main CI and normal guarded revision before read-only LAB acceptance. No LAB PASS from local/CI. No real message/upload/read acknowledgement/mail, payment/stock/shift/return/fiscal/financial-row change, production DDL, provider/config activation, or accepted transaction replay.
